	option = {#estate_madams_open_pirates
		name = cosmo_events.10.14
		trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_pirates_built
			any_owned_province = {
				# any_privateering_country = {
				# 	capital_scope = {
				# 		OR = {
				# 			continent = europe
				# 			continent = africa
				# 			superregion = china_superregion
				# 			superregion = east_indies_superregion
				# 		}
				# 	}
				# }
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
				# any_privateering_country = {
				# 	capital_scope = {
				# 		OR = {
				# 			continent = europe
				# 			continent = africa
				# 			superregion = china_superregion
				# 			superregion = east_indies_superregion
				# 		}
				# 	}
				# }
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
			}				
			}			
		}
		}
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_prostitute_menu
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_estate_privilege = estate_madams_open_pirates
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_privilege_already_active_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
				}
				any_privateering_country = {
					capital_scope = {
						OR = {
							continent = europe
							continent = africa
							superregion = china_superregion
							superregion = east_indies_superregion
						}
					}
				}				
				privateer_power = {
					#country = PREV
					share = 1
				}			
			}
			}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_no_pirates_near_port_of_pleasure_tt
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
				has_estate_privilege = estate_madams_open_pirates
				}
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
				}
				any_privateering_country = {
					capital_scope = {
						OR = {
							continent = europe
							continent = africa
							superregion = china_superregion
							superregion = east_indies_superregion
						}
					}
				}				
				privateer_power = {
					#country = PREV
					share = 1
				}			
			}				
			}
			hidden_effect = {
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_promote_prostitution		
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_closed_pirates		
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_tax_free_brothels		
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_relegate_prostitution
			}
			set_estate_privilege = 	estate_madams_open_pirates	
		}
	}
	option = {#estate_madams_closed_pirates
		name = cosmo_events.10.15
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_pirates_built
			any_province = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed	
			}			
		}
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_prostitute_menu
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_estate_privilege = estate_madams_closed_pirates
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_privilege_already_active_tt
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
				has_estate_privilege = estate_madams_closed_pirates
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_promote_prostitution		
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_open_pirates		
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_tax_free_brothels		
			remove_estate_privilege = estate_madams_relegate_prostitution	
			}

# prostitute Menu
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_events.11
	title = cosmo_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_events.11.d
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {

		}
	}
				
	option = {#estate_madams_ruler_visit
		name = cosmo_events.11.11
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					culture_group = andean_group		
					culture_group = aridoamerican		
					culture_group = otomanguean		
					culture_group = je_tupi		
					culture_group = je		
					culture_group = maranon		
					culture_group = chibchan		
					culture_group = carribean
					culture_group = maya		
					culture_group = central_american		

					culture_group = mataco		
					culture_group = araucanian		
					culture_group = eskaleut		
					culture_group = central_algonquian		
					culture_group = plains_algonquian		
					culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
					culture_group = iroquoian		
					culture_group = siouan		
					culture_group = caddoan		
					culture_group = muskogean		
					culture_group = sonoran		
					culture_group = apachean		
					culture_group = na_dene		
					culture_group = penutian


				}
			}
			set_country_flag = new_world_europe_brothels
		}
			set_country_flag = pagan_brothels
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_neighbor_country = {
				any_owned_province = {
						OR = {
							religion = confucianism
							religion_group = christian
							religion_group = muslim
						}
						OR = {
							province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1 
							has_building = brothel_district
						}									
						}			
					}
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						any_owned_province = {
						OR = {
							religion = confucianism
							religion_group = christian
							religion_group = muslim
						}
						OR = {
							province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1 
							has_building = brothel_district
						}									
						}
					any_known_country = {
						any_owned_province = {
							province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1 
						OR = {
							religion = confucianism
							religion_group = christian
							religion_group = muslim
						}
						OR = {
							controlled_by = ROOT
							is_strongest_trade_power = ROOT 
						}											
						}
					}
					}					
				}						
				}
				add_stability = -1 
			}
	}
	option = {#estate_madams_hire_madams
		name = cosmo_events.11.12
		
	}		
}

# Cosmopolitan 1
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_events.20
	title = cosmo_events.20.t
	desc = cosmo_events.20.d
	picture = cosmogfx_COSMOPOLITAN_eventPicture
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				 has_building = brothel_district 
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = french_prostitutes
					has_province_modifier = central_european_prostitutes
					has_province_modifier = iberian_prostitutes
					has_province_modifier = italian_prostitutes
					has_province_modifier = turkish_prostitutes
					has_province_modifier = british_prostitutes
					has_province_modifier = french_prostitutes
					has_province_modifier = russian_prostitutes
				}
				has_province_flag = cosmo_anathanism_origin 
			}
			save_event_target_as = prostituteprovince
		}				
			}
			}

	option = {#female advisor settings
		name = cosmo_events.20.a
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_events.21
			}				
	}
	option = {#view tutorial
		name = cosmo_events.20.b
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_events.4
			}
	}
	option = {#tutorial on
		name = cosmo_events.20.7
		trigger = {
			has_global_flag = cosmo_no_tutorials	
		}
		clr_global_flag = cosmo_no_tutorials
	}	
	option = {#tutorial off
		name = cosmo_events.20.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = cosmo_no_tutorials	
			}
		}		
		set_global_flag = cosmo_no_tutorials
	}
	option = {#fix religions
		name = cosmo_events.20.8
		trigger = {
			any_known_country = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					religion_group = pagan
					religion_group = christian
					religion_group = muslim
					religion_group = eastern
					religion_group = dharmic
					religion_group = jewish_group
					religion_group = zoroastrian_group
				}
				OR = {
					religion = ROOT
					capital_scope = {
						owner = {
							religion = PREV
						}
					}	
				}
			}				
			}
		}		
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						religion_group = pagan
						religion_group = christian
						religion_group = muslim
						religion_group = eastern
						religion_group = dharmic
						religion_group = jewish_group
						religion_group = zoroastrian_group
					}
					OR = {
						religion = ROOT
						capital_scope = {
							owner = {
								religion = PREV
							}
						}	
					}
				}
			}
				capital_scope = {
					owner = {
						change_religion = PREV
					}
				}		
		}
	}	
	option = {#add women generals
		name = cosmo_events.20.28
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_women_generals
			}
		}
		add_country_modifier = { 
			name = cosmo_women_generals
			duration =  -1
			}							
	}
	option = {#add women generals
		name = cosmo_events.20.29
		trigger = {
				has_country_modifier = cosmo_women_generals
		}
		remove_country_modifier = cosmo_women_generals			
	}
	option = {#support Anathanists
	name = cosmo_madamsd_events.5.a
	goto = prostituteprovince
	highlight = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_modifier = cosmo_anathanism_active
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_et_running
			religion = anathanism_protestant
		}
	}
	add_country_modifier = {
		name = cosmo_anathanism_active
		duration = -1
	}
	add_country_modifier = {
		name = cosmo_anathanism_start
		duration = 365
	}
		enable_religion = anathanism_protestant
		change_religion = anathanism_protestant		
		event_target:prostituteprovince = {
			change_religion = anathanism_protestant
			add_permanent_province_modifier = {
				name = "religious_zeal_at_conv"
				duration = 9000
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_propogate_religion_active_tt
			set_province_flag = cosmo_converting_repeater
			add_trade_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				duration = 5475
				power = 15
				key = IMMENSELY_POPULAR_PORT
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_convert_repeater
				duration = 5400  
				hidden = yes

			}					
		}
			add_estate_influence_modifier = {
					estate = estate_madams
					desc = COSMO_ADMINISTRATIVE_CONCESSIONS
					influence = -50
					duration = 1825
				}					
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_province = {
						has_cardinal = yes 
					}
				}
				every_owned_province = {
					limit = {
						has_cardinal = yes 
					}
					spawn_rebels = {
						type = catholic_rebels
						size = 2
						win = yes
					}				
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						any_owned_province = {
							has_cardinal = yes 
						}
					}

				}
				add_estate_loyalty = {
					estate = estate_church
					loyalty = -15
				}
			}		
			hidden_effect = {
			change_variable = {
			which = cosmoAnathanistCounter
			value = 20
			}
			if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
				which = cosmoAnathanistCounter	
				value = 480
				}
				}
			set_variable = {
			which = cosmoAnathanistCounter
			value = 0
				}
			}
		}									
	}	
	option = {#ET compatability
		name = cosmo_events.20.30
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_et_running
			}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = cosmo_et_running
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = cosmo_et_running
		}				
	}
	option = {#ET compatability off
		name = cosmo_events.20.36
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_et_running	
			any_province = {
				region = japan_region
				NOT = {
					culture = ainu
				}
				NOT = {
					culture = japanese
				}
			}					
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_province = {
					region = japan_region
					NOT = {
						culture = ainu
					}
					NOT = {
						culture = japanese
					}
				}
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					region = japan_region
					NOT = {
						culture = ainu
					}
					NOT = {
						culture = japanese
					}					
				}
				change_culture = japanese
			}
		}				
	}	
	option = {#ET compatability off
		name = cosmo_events.20.31
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_et_running			
		}
		if = {

			}
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_et_running
		}

							province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 2

									}

				}

	id = cosmo_events.1001
	title = cosmo_events.1001.t
	desc = cosmo_events.1001.d
	picture = cosmogfx_SWORD_DANCE_NUDE_eventPicture
	
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
			NOT = {	
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_recruit_dance
			}
		check_variable = {
			which = cosmoDegeneracyVariable
			value = 100
			}			
		any_state = {  
		has_state_edict = edict_promote_military_recruitment
		}
		}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
	}
	
	option = {
	name = cosmo_events.1001.a 
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_recruit_dance
		add_treasury = -50
		add_manpower = 5
		add_sailors = 50 		
	}
	option = {
	name = cosmo_events.1001.b 
	}
